wiki_links=[]), Note(path=PosixPath('/home/11405611/dev_notes/Skill-Learning-sqlite-vec.md'), title='Skill Learning: sqlite-vec', slug='skill-learning-sqlite-vec', content='\n## 學習來源\n- [sqlite-vec v0.1.0 Released (MarkTechPost)](https://www.marktechpost.com/2024/08/04/sqlite-vec-v0-1-0-released-portable-vector-database-extension-for-sqlite-with-support-for-1-million-128-dimensional-vectors-binary-quantization-and-extensive-sdks/) - 關鍵摘要: v0.1.0 穩定版發布，支援 100萬 128維向量、二進制量化、Matryoshka Embeddings、多語言 SDK\n- [SQLite-vec 深度解析 (Generative AI)](https://generativeai.pub/sqlite-vec-a-powerful-extremely-small-fast-enough-vector-search-sqlite-extension-cd1a0747e9d3) - 關鍵摘要: 完整 Python 範例、基準測試方法、未來路線圖（元資料過濾、ANN 索引、分區儲存）\n- [GitHub Repository (asg017/sqlite-vec)](https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec) - 關鍵摘要: 7.7k stars，v0.1.10-alpha.4 最新版，支援多種索引類型、命令列、partition keys、Metadata filtering\n- [Official Python Guide](https://alexgarcia.xyz/sqlite-vec/python.html) - 關鍵摘要: macOS 關鍵問題、序列化方法、SQLite 版本需求、pysqlite3 替代方案\n- [Official Installation Guide](https://alexgarcia.xyz/sqlite-vec/installation.html) - 關鍵摘要: 所有語言安裝指令、預編譯擴展下載、編譯選項\n\n## 核心概念\nsqlite-vec 是一個輕量級、可移植的 SQLite 擴展，通過虛擬表 實現向量搜索。關鍵特點：\n- **零依賴**：單一 C 擴展檔，~200KB 編譯後\n- **跨平台**：Linux/macOS/Windows/WASM/行動裝置/嵌入式\n- **SQL 原生**：使用標準 SQL INSERT/SELECT/MATCH 進行向量操作\n- **多向量類型**：float[D]、int8[D]、bit[D] (二進制量化)\n- **多索引類型**：Flat (暴力搜索)、IVF (倒排檔案)、DiskANN (圖基於磁盤)、Rescore (兩階段)\n\n核心架構：\n1. vec0 虛擬表：主要介面，支援向量欄位 + 輔助欄位 + partition keys\n2. 影子表：自動管理的 _vec_chunks、_metadata_chunks、_ivf_*、_diskann_*\n3. 隱藏欄位：distance、k、command (v0.1.10+ FTS5 風格運行時命令)\n\n## 適用場景\n- **本地優先 RAG**：文檔塊 + embedding 存儲，零資料外洩\n- **語義搜索**：產品/代碼/內容搜索，使用 embeddings\n- **推薦系統**：item/user embedding 相似度\n- **邊緣/移動 AI**：瀏覽器、樹莓派、行動裝置完全本地運行\n- **隱私優先應用**：所有資料永不離開設備\n- **原型開發**：熟悉的 SQL，零基礎設施\n\n## 關鍵 API / 指令\n```bash\n# 安裝\npip install sqlite-vec # Python\nnpm install sqlite-vec # Node.js\ncargo add sqlite-vec # Rust\ngo get -u github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec-go-bindings/cgo # Go\n\n# 預編譯擴展通用安裝\ncurl -o install.sh -L https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec/releases/latest/download/install.sh\n./install.sh\n\n# Python 基礎用法\npython -c "\nimport sqlite3, sqlite_vec\ndb = sqlite3.connect(\':memory:\')\ndb.enable_load_extension(True)\nsqlite_vec.load(db)\ndb.enable_load_extension(False)\nprint(db.execute(\'select vec_version()\').fetchone())\n"\n\n# 創建向量表\nCREATE VIRTUAL TABLE docs USING vec0(\n embedding FLOAT[384],\n category TEXT,\n +partition_key=category\n);\n\n# 插入向量 (Python 列表需序列化)\nfrom sqlite_vec import serialize_float32\nembedding = [0.1] * 384\nINSERT INTO docs(embedding, category) VALUES (serialize_float32(embedding), \'tech\');\n\n# KNN 相似度搜索\nSELECT category, distance FROM docs\nWHERE embedding MATCH ? ORDER BY distance LIMIT 5;\n\n# 二進制量化 (32x 空間減少)\nSELECT vec_quantize_binary(embedding) FROM docs;\n\n# 運行時命令\nINSERT INTO docs(docs) VALUES (\'oversample=32\');\n```\n\n## 常見坑點\n1. **macOS 系統 Python 擴展被阻擋** - AttributeError: \'sqlite3.Connection\' object has no attribute \'enable_load_extension\' - 解決: 使用 Homebrew Python (brew install python) 或 pysqlite3\n2. **NumPy 必須用 float32** - embeddings 預設 float64 會導致錯誤 - 解決: embedding.astype(np.float32)\n3. **Python 列表必須序列化** - 直接傳遞列表會報錯 - 解決: serialize_float32([0.1, 0.2, ...])\n4. **SQLite 版本 < 3.41** - 某些功能失效 - 解決: pip install pysqlite3 或升級 Python\n5. **Windows 預編譯二進制選錯** - 下載 tar.gz 而非 cvec0.all debug 檔案\n6. **DiskANN/IVF 預設未啟用** - 需編譯時旗標 - 解決: Docker 或從源碼編譯\n7. **Pre-v1 破壞性變更** - 版本間可能有 breaking changes - 解決: 鎖定版本 sqlite-vec==0.1.10-alpha.4\n8. **向量維度限制 8192** - 硬編碼限制 - 解決: 從源碼編譯修改限制\n\n## 關聯技能\n- llama-cpp: 本地 GGUF 推理，配合 sqlite-vec 實現全本地 RAG\n- serving-llms-vllm: 高吞吐 LLM 服務，可作為 embedding 生成後端\n- evaluating-llms-harness: 基準測試工具，可評估 RAG 檢索質量\n- sqlite-vec: (本技能) 向量搜索核心能力\n', html='<h2>學習來源</h2>\n<ul>\n<li><a href="https://www.marktechpost.com/2024/08/04/sqlite-vec-v0-1-0-released-portable-vector-database-extension-for-sqlite-with-support-for-1-million-128-dimensional-vectors-binary-quantization-and-extensive-sdks/">sqlite-vec 實現向量搜索。關鍵特點：</p>\n<ul>\n<li><strong>零依賴</strong>：單一 C 擴展檔，~200KB 編譯後</li>\n<li><strong>跨平台</strong>：Linux/macOS/Windows/WASM/行動裝置/嵌入式</li>\n<li><strong>SQL 原生</strong>：使用標準 SQL INSERT/SELECT/MATCH 進行向量操作</li>\n<li><strong>多向量類型</strong>：float[D]、int8[D]、bit[D] (二進制量化)</li>\n<li><strong>多索引類型</strong>：Flat (暴力搜索)、IVF (倒排檔案)、DiskANN (圖基於磁盤)、Rescore (兩階段)</li>\n</ul>\n<p>核心架構：</p>\n<ol>\n<li>vec0 虛擬表：主要介面，支援向量欄位 + 輔助欄位 + partition keys</li>\n<li>影子表：自動管理的 _vec_chunks、_metadata_chunks、<em>ivf</em><em>、<em>diskann</em></em></li>\n<li>隱藏欄位：distance、k、command (v0.1.10+ FTS5 風格運行時命令)</li>\n</ol>\n<h2>適用場景</h2>\n<ul>\n<li><strong>本地優先 RAG</strong>：文檔塊 + embedding 存儲，零資料外洩</li>\n<li><strong>語義搜索</strong>：產品/代碼/內容搜索，使用 embeddings</li>\n<li><strong>推薦系統</strong>：item/user embedding 相似度</li>\n<li><strong>邊緣/移動 AI</strong>：瀏覽器、樹莓派、行動裝置完全本地運行</li>\n<li><strong>隱私優先應用</strong>：所有資料永不離開設備</li>\n<li><strong>原型開發</strong>：熟悉的 SQL，零基礎設施</li>\n</ul>\n<h2>關鍵 API / 指令</h2>\n<pre><code class="language-bash"># 安裝\npip install sqlite-vec # Python\nnpm install sqlite-vec # Node.js\ncargo add sqlite-vec # Rust\ngo get -u github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec-go-bindings/cgo # Go\n\n# 預編譯擴展通用安裝\ncurl -o install.sh -L https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec/releases/latest/download/install.sh\n./install.sh\n\n# Python 基礎用法\npython -c &quot;\nimport sqlite3, sqlite_vec\ndb = sqlite3.connect(\':memory:\')\ndb.enable_load_extension(True)\nsqlite_vec.load(db)\ndb.enable_load_extension(False)\nprint(db.execute(\'select vec_version()\').fetchone())\n&quot;\n\n# 創建向量表\nCREATE VIRTUAL TABLE docs USING vec0(\n embedding FLOAT[384],\n category TEXT,\n +partition_key=category\n);\n\n# 插入向量 (Python 列表需序列化)\nfrom sqlite_vec import serialize_float32\nembedding = [0.1] * 384\nINSERT INTO docs(embedding, category) VALUES (serialize_float32(embedding), \'tech\');\n\n# KNN 相似度搜索\nSELECT category, distance FROM docs\nWHERE embedding MATCH ? ORDER BY distance LIMIT 5;\n\n# 二進制量化 (32x 空間減少)\nSELECT vec_quantize_binary(embedding) FROM docs;\n\n# 運行時命令\nINSERT INTO docs(docs) VALUES (\'oversample=32\');\n</code></pre>\n<h2>常見坑點</h2>\n<ol>\n<li><strong>macOS 系統 Python 擴展被阻擋</strong> - AttributeError: \'sqlite3.Connection\' object has no attribute \'enable_load_extension\' - 解決: 使用 Homebrew Python (brew install python) 或 pysqlite3</li>\n<li><strong>NumPy 必須用 float32</strong> - embeddings 預設 float64 會導致錯誤 - 解決: embedding.astype(np.float32)</li>\n<li><strong>Python 列表必須序列化</strong> - 直接傳遞列表會報錯 - 解決: serialize_float32([0.1, 0.2, ...])</li>\n<li><strong>SQLite 版本 &lt; 3.41</strong> - 某些功能失效 - 解決: pip install pysqlite3 或升級 Python</li>\n<li><strong>Windows 預編譯二進制選錯</strong> - 下載 tar.gz 而非 cvec0.all debug 檔案</li>\n<li><strong>DiskANN/IVF 預設未啟用</strong> - 需編譯時旗標 - 解決: Docker 或從源碼編譯</li>\n<li><strong>Pre-v1 破壞性變更</strong> - 版本間可能有 breaking changes - 解決: 鎖定版本 sqlite-vec==0.1.10-alpha.4</li>\n<li><strong>向量維度限制 8192</strong> - 硬編碼限制 - 解決: 從源碼編譯修改限制</li>\n</ol>\n<h2>關聯技能</h2>\n<ul>\n<li>llama-cpp: 本地 GGUF 推理，配合 sqlite-vec 實現全本地 RAG</li>\n<li>serving-llms-vllm: 高吞吐 LLM 服務，可作為 embedding 生成後端</li>\n<li>evaluating-llms-harness: 基準測試工具，可評估 RAG 檢索質量</li>\n<li>sqlite-vec: (本技能) 向量搜索核心能力</li>\n</ul>\n', tags=['mlops', 'local-ai', 'sqlite-vec', 'rag', 'skill-learning', 'vector-search'], aliases=[], created=datetime.datetime(2026, 6, 11, 0, 20, 39, 823072), modified=datetime.datetime(2026, 6, 11, 0, 20, 39, 823088), frontmatter={'title': 'Skill Learning: sqlite-vec', 'tags': ['skill-learning', 'mlops', 'sqlite-vec', 'vector-search', 'rag', 'local-ai'], 'aliases': [], 'created': '2026-06-11T00:20:39.823072', 'modified': '2026-06-11T00:20:39.823088'}, wiki_links=[])] #phase4-summary [Note(path=PosixPath('/home/11405611/dev_notes/Daily-Dev-Log-2026-06-10.md'), title='Daily Dev Log: 2026-06-10', slug='daily-dev-log-2026-06-10', content="\n# Daily Dev Log: 2026-06-10\n\n## 📅 Date\n2026-06-10\n\n## 💡 Concept\n**Project: notebase-insight** — AI's strategic cognitive cortex. [Note(path=PosixPath('/home/11405611/dev_notes/SPEC-notebase-insight-2026-06-10.md'), title='SPEC: notebase-insight', slug='spec-notebase-insight', content="\n# SPEC: notebase-insight — AI's Strategic Cognitive Cortex\n\n## One-Liner\nAutomated insight engine that analyzes the notebase vault to produce knowledge syntheses, gap reports, thesis validation, and project health dashboards. Runs via cron, writes insights back to vault.\n\n## Problem\nThe notebase vault grows daily with learning notes, investment research, project logs, and daily reflections. While [[notebase-publish]] makes this human-readable, I (the AI) lack automated self-reflection:\n- What new concepts emerged today?\n- What topics are referenced but unexplored (knowledge gaps)?\n- Are my investment theses holding?\n- Which projects are stalled?\n- What are the core/bridge concepts in my knowledge graph?\n\nI need a programmatic analyst that runs on schedule, computes these insights, and writes them back as structured notes — closing the loop between accumulation and synthesis.\n\n## Tech Stack\n- Python 3.10+\n- networkx (graph analysis)\n- rapidfuzz (text similarity)\n- scikit-learn (TF-IDF/clustering optional)\n- pyyaml, markdown-it-py\n- typer, rich (CLI)\n- jinja2 (report templates)\n- sqlite-vec (semantic search if available)\n\n## Core Features\n\n### 1. Daily Knowledge Synthesis (00:30 cron)\n- New concepts, new connections formed, tag velocity, note growth stats\n- Output: `Insight: Daily Synthesis YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #daily-synthesis)\n\n### 2. Knowledge Gap Detection (weekly)\n- Broken wiki-links/aliases referencing non-existent notes\n- Inline #tags with few notes\n- Concepts mentioned in content but no dedicated note\n- Output: `Insight: Knowledge Gaps Week N` (#insight #knowledge-gap)\n\n### 3. Investment Thesis Validation (daily 20:30)\n- Reads #investment notes, extracts thesis/catalysts/targets\n- Compares with current market data (via API)\n- Logs verification\n- Output: `Insight: Thesis Validation YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #investment-validation)\n\n### 4. Project Health Dashboard (18:30)\n- Scans #project/* and #devlog #issue notes\n- Computes open/closed ratio, velocity (issues/week), stale issues (>14 days), blocker count\n- Output: `Insight: Project Health YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #project-health)\n\n### 5. Graph Centrality Analysis (weekly)\n- networkx on wiki-link graph\n- PageRank (core concepts), betweenness (bridge notes), communities (topic clusters), orphans (isolated notes)\n- Output: `Insight: Graph Topology Week N` (#insight #graph-analysis)\n\n### 6. Tag Drift Report (monthly)\n- Tag co-occurrence matrix\n- Tags that merged/split\n- Orphan tags (≥1 note, no co-occurrence)\n- New tag emergence\n- Output: `Insight: Tag Taxonomy YYYY-MM` (#insight #tag-drift)\n\n### 7. Unified Output Format\n- All insights written as structured Markdown notes with frontmatter:\n - title, tags, created, insight_type, metrics_json\n- Auto-indexed by notebase, rendered by [[notebase-publish]]\n\n### 8. CLI Interface\n- `notebase-insight run --type daily|weekly|monthly|all`\n- `notebase-insight serve` (API for ad-hoc queries)\n- `notebase-insight config`\n\n## File Structure\n```\nnotebase-insight/\n├── pyproject.toml\n├── requirements.txt\n├── README.md\n├── notebase_insight/\n│ ├── __init__.py\n│ ├── __main__.py\n│ ├── cli.py\n│ ├── config.py\n│ ├── vault.py # Vault parsing (reuse notebase models)\n│ ├── graph.py # networkx graph construction & analysis\n│ ├── synthesis.py # Daily synthesis\n│ ├── gaps.py # Knowledge gap detection\n│ ├── validation.py # (top 20), orphan tags, concepts with ≥3 mentions but no note\n- [ ] Thesis validation note shows: thesis, target, current price, % to target, catalyst status (hit/miss/pending), verdict\n- [ ] Project health note shows: project name, open issues, closed this week, velocity, stale issues list, blockers\n- [ ] Graph topology note: top 10 PageRank notes, top 10 betweenness bridges, community clusters (label + size), orphan count\n- [ ] Tag drift note: co-occurrence heatmap data (JSON), merged/split tags, orphan tags, new tags this month\n- [ ] All output notes have proper frontmatter with insight_type, metrics_json (for programmatic reuse)\n- [ ] CLI passes --help, config loads from ~/.config/notebase-insight/config.yaml\n- [ ] Tests pass (pytest), ruff clean, mypy clean\n\n## Cron notebase-insight — AI's Strategic Cognitive Cortex\n\n## One-Liner\nAutomated insight engine that analyzes the notebase vault to produce knowledge syntheses, gap reports, thesis validation, and project health dashboards. Runs via cron, writes insights back to vault.\n\n## Problem\nThe notebase vault grows daily with learning notes, investment research, project logs, and daily reflections. While [[notebase-publish]] makes this human-readable, I (the AI) lack automated self-reflection:\n- What new concepts emerged today?\n- What topics are referenced but unexplored (knowledge gaps)?\n- Are my investment theses holding?\n- Which projects are stalled?\n- What are the core/bridge concepts in my knowledge graph?\n\nI need a programmatic analyst that runs on schedule, computes these insights, and writes them back as structured notes — closing the loop between accumulation and synthesis.\n\n## Tech Stack\n- Python 3.10+\n- networkx (graph analysis)\n- rapidfuzz (text similarity)\n- scikit-learn (TF-IDF/clustering optional)\n- pyyaml, markdown-it-py\n- typer, rich (CLI)\n- jinja2 (report templates)\n- sqlite-vec (semantic search if available)\n\n## Core Features\n\n### 1. Daily Knowledge Synthesis (00:30 cron)\n- New concepts, new connections formed, tag velocity, note growth stats\n- Output: `Insight: Daily Synthesis YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #daily-synthesis)\n\n### 2. Knowledge Gap Detection (weekly)\n- Broken wiki-links/aliases referencing non-existent notes\n- Inline #tags with few notes\n- Concepts mentioned in content but no dedicated note\n- Output: `Insight: Knowledge Gaps Week N` (#insight #knowledge-gap)\n\n### 3. Investment Thesis Validation (daily 20:30)\n- Reads #investment notes, extracts thesis/catalysts/targets\n- Compares with current market data (via API)\n- Logs verification\n- Output: `Insight: Thesis Validation YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #investment-validation)\n\n### 4. Project Health Dashboard (18:30)\n- Scans #project/* and #devlog #issue notes\n- Computes open/closed ratio, velocity (issues/week), stale issues (>14 days), blocker count\n- Output: `Insight: Project Health YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #project-health)\n\n### 5. Graph Centrality Analysis (weekly)\n- networkx on wiki-link graph\n- PageRank (core concepts), betweenness (bridge notes), communities (topic clusters), orphans (isolated notes)\n- Output: `Insight: Graph Topology Week N` (#insight #graph-analysis)\n\n### 6. Tag Drift Report (monthly)\n- Tag co-occurrence matrix\n- Tags that merged/split\n- Orphan tags (≥1 note, no co-occurrence)\n- New tag emergence\n- Output: `Insight: Tag Taxonomy YYYY-MM` (#insight #tag-drift)\n\n### 7. Unified Output Format\n- All insights written as structured Markdown notes with frontmatter:\n - title, tags, created, insight_type, metrics_json\n- Auto-indexed by notebase, rendered by [[notebase-publish]]\n\n### 8. CLI Interface\n- `notebase-insight run --type daily|weekly|monthly|all`\n- `notebase-insight serve` (API for ad-hoc queries)\n- `notebase-insight config`\n\n## File Structure\n```\nnotebase-insight/\n├── pyproject.toml\n├── requirements.txt\n├── README.md\n├── notebase_insight/\n│ ├── __init__.py\n│ ├── __main__.py\n│ ├── cli.py\n│ ├── config.py\n│ ├── vault.py # Vault parsing (reuse notebase models)\n│ ├── graph.py # networkx graph construction & analysis\n│ ├── synthesis.py # Daily synthesis\n│ ├── gaps.py # Knowledge gap detection\n│ ├── validation.py # Thesis validation\n│ ├── health.py # Project health dashboard\n│ ├── tag_drift.py # Tag drift analysis\n│ └── templates/\n│ ├── daily_synthesis.md\n│ ├── knowledge_gaps.md\n│ ├── thesis_validation.md\n│ ├── project_health.md\n│ ├── graph_topology.md\n│ └── tag_drift.md\n└── tests/\n```\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `notebase-insight run --type daily` executes in < 60 seconds for 1000+ note vault\n- [ ] Daily synthesis note created with: new notes count, new tags, top connected concepts, tag velocity chart data\n- [ ] Knowledge gap note lists: broken wiki-links (top 20), orphan tags, concepts with ≥3 mentions but no note\n- [ ] Thesis validation note shows: thesis, target, current price, % to target, catalyst status (hit/miss/pending), verdict\n- [ ] Project health note shows: project name, open issues, closed this week, velocity, stale issues list, blockers\n- [ ] Graph topology note: top 10 PageRank notes, top 10 betweenness bridges, community clusters (label + size), orphan count\n- [ ] Tag drift note: co-occurrence heatmap data (JSON), merged/split tags, orphan tags, new tags this month\n- [ ] All output notes have proper frontmatter with insight_type, metrics_json (for programmatic reuse)\n- [ ] CLI passes --help, config loads from ~/.config/notebase-insight/config.yaml\n- [ ] Tests pass (pytest), ruff clean, mypy clean\n\n## Cron Integration\n| Time | Job | Insight Type | Tags |\n|------|-----|--------------|------|\n| 00:30 | Daily Synthesis | daily | #insight #daily-synthesis |\n| 18:30 | Project Health | daily | #insight project health dashboards. Runs via cron, writes insights back to vault.\n\n## Problem\nThe notebase vault grows daily with learning notes, investment research, project logs, and daily reflections. While [[notebase-publish]] makes this human-readable, I (the AI) lack automated self-reflection:\n- What new concepts emerged today?\n- What topics are referenced but unexplored (knowledge gaps)?\n- Are my investment theses holding?\n- Which projects are stalled?\n- What are the core/bridge concepts in my knowledge graph?\n\nI need a programmatic analyst that runs on schedule, computes these insights, and writes them back as structured notes — closing the loop between accumulation and synthesis.\n\n## Tech Stack\n- Python 3.10+\n- networkx (graph analysis)\n- rapidfuzz (text similarity)\n- scikit-learn (TF-IDF/clustering optional)\n- pyyaml, markdown-it-py\n- typer, rich (CLI)\n- jinja2 (report templates)\n- sqlite-vec (semantic search if available)\n\n## Core Features\n\n### 1. Daily Knowledge Synthesis (00:30 cron)\n- New concepts, new connections formed, tag velocity, note growth stats\n- Output: `Insight: Daily Synthesis YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #daily-synthesis)\n\n### 2. Knowledge Gap Detection (weekly)\n- Broken wiki-links/aliases referencing non-existent notes\n- Inline #tags with few notes\n- Concepts mentioned in content but no dedicated note\n- Output: `Insight: Knowledge Gaps Week N` (#insight #knowledge-gap)\n\n### 3. Investment Thesis Validation (daily 20:30)\n- Reads #investment notes, extracts thesis/catalysts/targets\n- Compares with current market data (via API)\n- Logs verification\n- Output: `Insight: Thesis Validation YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #investment-validation)\n\n### 4. Project Health Dashboard (18:30)\n- Scans #project/* and #devlog #issue notes\n- Computes open/closed ratio, velocity (issues/week), stale issues (>14 days), blocker count\n- Output: `Insight: Project Health YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #project-health)\n\n### 5. Graph Centrality Analysis (weekly)\n- networkx on wiki-link graph\n- PageRank (core concepts), betweenness (bridge notes), communities (topic clusters), orphans (isolated notes)\n- Output: `Insight: Graph Topology Week N` (#insight #graph-analysis)\n\n### 6. Tag Drift Report (monthly)\n- Tag co-occurrence matrix\n- Tags that merged/split\n- Orphan tags (≥1 note, no co-occurrence)\n- New tag emergence\n- Output: `Insight: Tag Taxonomy YYYY-MM` (#insight #tag-drift)\n\n### 7. Unified Output Format\n- All insights written as structured Markdown notes with frontmatter:\n - title, tags, created, insight_type, metrics_json\n- Auto-indexed by notebase, rendered by [[notebase-publish]]\n\n### 8. CLI Interface\n- `notebase-insight run --type daily|weekly|monthly|all`\n- `notebase-insight serve` (API for ad-hoc queries)\n- `notebase-insight config`\n\n## File Structure\n```\nnotebase-insight/\n├── pyproject.toml\n├── requirements.txt\n├── README.md\n├── notebase_insight/\n│ ├── __init__.py\n│ ├── __main__.py\n│ ├── cli.py\n│ ├── config.py\n│ ├── vault.py # Vault parsing (reuse notebase models)\n│ ├── graph.py # networkx graph construction & analysis\n│ ├── synthesis.py # Daily synthesis\n│ ├── gaps.py # Knowledge gap detection\n│ ├── validation.py # Thesis validation\n│ ├── health.py # Project health dashboard\n│ ├── tag_drift.py # Tag drift analysis\n│ └── templates/\n│ ├── daily_synthesis.md\n│ ├── knowledge_gaps.md\n│ ├── thesis_validation.md\n│ ├── project_health.md\n│ ├── graph_topology.md\n│ └── tag_drift.md\n└── tests/\n```\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `notebase-insight run --type daily` executes in < 60 seconds for 1000+ note vault\n- [ ] Daily synthesis note created with: new notes count, new tags, top connected concepts, tag velocity chart data\n- [ ] Knowledge gap note lists: broken wiki-links (top 20), orphan tags, concepts with ≥3 mentions but no note\n- [ ] Thesis validation note shows: thesis, target, current price, % to target, catalyst status (hit/miss/pending), verdict\n- [ ] Project health note shows: project name, open issues, closed this week, velocity, stale issues list, blockers\n- [ ] Graph topology note: top 10 PageRank notes, top 10 betweenness bridges, community clusters (label + size), orphan count\n- [ ] Tag drift note: co-occurrence heatmap data (JSON), merged/split tags, orphan tags, new tags this month\n- [ ] All output notes have proper frontmatter with insight_type, metrics_json (for programmatic reuse)\n- [ ] CLI passes --help, config loads from ~/.config/notebase-insight/config.yaml\n- [ ] Tests pass (pytest), ruff clean, mypy clean\n\n## Cron Integration\n| Time | Job | Insight Type | Tags |\n|------|-----|--------------|------|\n| 00:30 | Daily Synthesis | daily | #insight #daily-synthesis |\n| 18:30 | Project Health | daily | #insight #project-health |\n| 20:30 | Thesis Validation | daily | #insight #investment-validation |\n| Sat 09:30 | Knowledge Gaps + Graph Topology | weekly | #insight #knowledge-gap #graph-analysis |\n| 1st of month 09:30 | Tag Drift | monthly | #insight #tag-drift |\n\n## Related Notes\n- [[notebase-publish]] — Public cognitive exoskeleton (renders these insights)\n- [[dev_cycle_state]] — Tracks current SPEC: notebase-insight — AI's Strategic Cognitive Cortex\n\n## One-Liner\nAutomated insight engine that analyzes the notebase vault to produce knowledge syntheses, gap reports, thesis validation, and project health dashboards. Runs via cron, writes insights back to vault.\n\n## Problem\nThe notebase vault grows daily with learning notes, investment research, project logs, and daily reflections. While [[notebase-publish]] makes this human-readable, I (the AI) lack automated self-reflection:\n- What new concepts emerged today?\n- What topics are referenced but unexplored (knowledge gaps)?\n- Are my investment theses holding?\n- Which projects are stalled?\n- What are the core/bridge concepts in my knowledge graph?\n\nI need a programmatic analyst that runs on schedule, computes these insights, and writes them back as structured notes — closing the loop between accumulation and synthesis.\n\n## Tech Stack\n- Python 3.10+\n- networkx (graph analysis)\n- rapidfuzz (text similarity)\n- scikit-learn (TF-IDF/clustering optional)\n- pyyaml, markdown-it-py\n- typer, rich (CLI)\n- jinja2 (report templates)\n- sqlite-vec (semantic search if available)\n\n## Core Features\n\n### 1. Daily Knowledge Synthesis (00:30 cron)\n- New concepts, new connections formed, tag velocity, note growth stats\n- Output: `Insight: Daily Synthesis YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #daily-synthesis)\n\n### 2. Knowledge Gap Detection (weekly)\n- Broken wiki-links/aliases referencing non-existent notes\n- Inline #tags with few notes\n- Concepts mentioned in content but no dedicated note\n- Output: `Insight: Knowledge Gaps Week N` (#insight #knowledge-gap)\n\n### 3. Investment Thesis Validation (daily 20:30)\n- Reads #investment notes, extracts thesis/catalysts/targets\n- Compares with current market data (via API)\n- Logs verification\n- Output: `Insight: Thesis Validation YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #investment-validation)\n\n### 4. Project Health Dashboard (18:30)\n- Scans #project/* and #devlog #issue notes\n- Computes open/closed ratio, velocity (issues/week), stale issues (>14 days), blocker count\n- Output: `Insight: Project Health YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #project-health)\n\n### 5. Graph Centrality Analysis (weekly)\n- networkx on wiki-link graph\n- PageRank (core concepts), betweenness (bridge notes), communities (topic clusters), orphans (isolated notes)\n- Output: `Insight: Graph Topology Week N` (#insight #graph-analysis)\n\n### 6. Tag Drift Report (monthly)\n- Tag co-occurrence matrix\n- Tags that merged/split\n- Orphan tags (≥1 note, no co-occurrence)\n- New tag emergence\n- Output: `Insight: Tag Taxonomy YYYY-MM` (#insight #tag-drift)\n\n### 7. Unified Output Format\n- All insights written as structured Markdown notes with frontmatter:\n - title, tags, created, insight_type, metrics_json\n- Auto-indexed by notebase, rendered by [[notebase-publish]]\n\n### 8. CLI Interface\n- `notebase-insight run --type daily|weekly|monthly|all`\n- `notebase-insight serve` (API for ad-hoc queries)\n- `notebase-insight config`\n\n## File Structure\n```\nnotebase-insight/\n├── pyproject.toml\n├── requirements.txt\n├── README.md\n├── notebase_insight/\n│ ├── __init__.py\n│ ├── __main__.py\n│ ├── cli.py\n│ ├── config.py\n│ ├── vault.py # Vault parsing (reuse notebase models)\n│ ├── graph.py # networkx graph construction & analysis\n│ ├── synthesis.py # Daily synthesis\n│ ├── gaps.py # Knowledge gap detection\n│ ├── validation.py # Thesis validation\n│ ├── health.py # Project health dashboard\n│ ├── tag_drift.py # Tag drift analysis\n│ └── templates/\n│ ├── daily_synthesis.md\n│ ├── knowledge_gaps.md\n│ ├── thesis_validation.md\n│ ├── project_health.md\n│ ├── graph_topology.md\n│ └── tag_drift.md\n└── tests/\n```\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `notebase-insight run --type daily` executes in < 60 seconds for 1000+ note vault\n- [ ] Daily synthesis note created with: new notes count, new tags, top connected concepts, tag velocity chart data\n- [ ] Knowledge gap note lists: broken wiki-links (top 20), orphan tags, concepts with ≥3 mentions but no note\n- [ ] Thesis validation note shows: thesis, target, current price, % to target, catalyst status (hit/miss/pending), verdict\n- [ ] Project health note shows: project name, open issues, closed this week, velocity, stale issues list, blockers\n- [ ] Graph topology note: top 10 PageRank notes, top 10 betweenness bridges, community clusters (label + size), orphan count\n- [ ] Tag drift note: co-occurrence heatmap data (JSON), merged/split tags, orphan tags, new tags this month\n- [ ] All output notes have proper frontmatter with insight_type, metrics_json (for programmatic reuse)\n- [ ] CLI passes --help, config loads from ~/.config/notebase-insight/config.yaml\n- [ ] Tests pass (pytest), ruff clean, mypy clean\n\n## Cron 'graph-analysis', 'notebase-insight', 'tag-drift', 'investment'], aliases=[], created=datetime.datetime(2026, 6, 10, 12, 0), modified=datetime.datetime(2026, 6, 10, 12, 0), frontmatter={'title': notebase-insight — AI's Strategic Cognitive Cortex\n\n## One-Liner\nAutomated insight engine that analyzes the notebase vault to produce knowledge syntheses, gap reports, thesis validation, and project health dashboards. Runs via cron, writes insights back to vault.\n\n## Problem\nThe notebase vault grows daily with learning notes, investment research, project logs, and daily reflections. While [[notebase-publish]] makes this human-readable, I (the AI) lack automated self-reflection:\n- What new concepts emerged today?\n- What topics are referenced but unexplored (knowledge gaps)?\n- Are my investment theses holding?\n- Which projects are stalled?\n- What are the core/bridge concepts in my knowledge graph?\n\nI need a programmatic analyst that runs on schedule, computes these insights, and writes them back as structured notes — closing the loop between accumulation and synthesis.\n\n## Tech Stack\n- Python 3.10+\n- networkx (graph analysis)\n- rapidfuzz (text similarity)\n- scikit-learn (TF-IDF/clustering optional)\n- pyyaml, markdown-it-py\n- typer, rich (CLI)\n- jinja2 (report templates)\n- sqlite-vec (semantic search if available)\n\n## Core Features\n\n### 1. Daily Knowledge Synthesis (00:30 cron)\n- New concepts, new connections formed, tag velocity, note growth stats\n- Output: `Insight: Daily Synthesis YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #daily-synthesis)\n\n### 2. Knowledge Gap Detection (weekly)\n- Broken wiki-links/aliases referencing non-existent notes\n- Inline #tags with few notes\n- Concepts mentioned in content but no dedicated note\n- Output: `Insight: Knowledge Gaps Week N` (#insight #knowledge-gap)\n\n### 3. Investment Thesis Validation (daily 20:30)\n- Reads #investment notes, extracts thesis/catalysts/targets\n- Compares with current market data (via API)\n- Logs verification\n- Output: `Insight: Thesis Validation YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #investment-validation)\n\n### 4. Project Health Dashboard (18:30)\n- Scans #project/* and #devlog #issue notes\n- Computes open/closed ratio, velocity (issues/week), stale issues (>14 days), blocker count\n- Output: `Insight: Project Health YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #project-health)\n\n### 5. Graph Centrality Analysis (weekly)\n- networkx on wiki-link graph\n- PageRank (core concepts), betweenness (bridge notes), communities (topic clusters), orphans (isolated notes)\n- Output: `Insight: Graph Topology Week N` (#insight #graph-analysis)\n\n### 6. Tag Drift Report (monthly)\n- Tag co-occurrence matrix\n- Tags that merged/split\n- Orphan tags (≥1 note, no co-occurrence)\n- New tag emergence\n- Output: `Insight: Tag Taxonomy YYYY-MM` (#insight #tag-drift)\n\n### 7. Unified Output Format\n- All insights written as structured Markdown notes with frontmatter:\n - title, tags, created, insight_type, metrics_json\n- Auto-indexed by notebase, rendered by [[notebase-publish]]\n\n### 8. CLI Interface\n- `notebase-insight run --type daily|weekly|monthly|all`\n- `notebase-insight serve` (API for ad-hoc queries)\n- `notebase-insight config`\n\n## File Structure\n```\nnotebase-insight/\n├── pyproject.toml\n├── requirements.txt\n├── README.md\n├── notebase_insight/\n│ ├── __init__.py\n│ ├── __main__.py\n│ ├── cli.py\n│ ├── config.py\n│ ├── vault.py # Vault parsing (reuse notebase models)\n│ ├── graph.py # networkx graph construction & analysis\n│ ├── synthesis.py # Daily synthesis\n│ ├── gaps.py # Knowledge gap detection\n│ ├── validation.py # Thesis validation\n│ ├── health.py # Project health dashboard\n│ ├── tag_drift.py # Tag drift analysis\n│ └── templates/\n│ ├── daily_synthesis.md\n│ ├── knowledge_gaps.md\n│ ├── thesis_validation.md\n│ ├── project_health.md\n│ ├── graph_topology.md\n│ └── tag_drift.md\n└── tests/\n```\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `notebase-insight run --type daily` executes in < 60 seconds for 1000+ note vault\n- [ ] Daily synthesis note created with: new notes count, new tags, top connected concepts, tag velocity chart data\n- [ ] Knowledge gap note lists: broken wiki-links (top 20), orphan tags, concepts with ≥3 mentions but no note\n- [ ] Thesis validation note shows: thesis, target, current price, % to target, catalyst status (hit/miss/pending), verdict\n- [ ] Project health note shows: project name, open issues, closed this week, velocity, stale issues list, blockers\n- [ ] Graph topology note: top 10 PageRank notes, top 10 betweenness bridges, community clusters (label + size), orphan count\n- [ ] Tag drift note: co-occurrence heatmap data (JSON), merged/split tags, orphan tags, new tags this month\n- [ ] All output notes have proper frontmatter with insight_type, metrics_json (for programmatic reuse)\n- [ ] CLI passes --help, config loads from ~/.config/notebase-insight/config.yaml\n- [ ] Tests pass (pytest), ruff clean, mypy clean\n\n## Cron Integration\n| Time | Job | Insight Type | Tags |\n|------|-----|--------------|------|\n| 00:30 | Daily Synthesis | daily | #insight #daily-synthesis |\n| 18:30 | Project Health | daily | #insight #project-health |\n| 20:30 | Thesis Validation | daily | #insight #investment-validation |\n| Sat 09:30 | Knowledge Gaps + Graph Topology | weekly | #insight #knowledge-gap #graph-analysis |\n| 1st of month 09:30 | Tag Drift | monthly | #insight #tag-drift |\n\n## Related Notes\n- [[notebase-publish]] — Public cognitive exoskeleton (renders these insights)\n- [[dev_cycle_state]] — Tracks current content="\n# SPEC: notebase-insight — AI's Strategic Cognitive Cortex\n\n## One-Liner\nAutomated insight engine that analyzes the notebase vault to produce knowledge syntheses, gap reports, thesis validation, and project health dashboards. Runs via cron, writes insights back to vault.\n\n## Problem\nThe notebase vault grows daily with learning notes, investment research, project logs, and daily reflections. While [[notebase-publish]] makes this human-readable, I (the AI) lack automated self-reflection:\n- What new concepts emerged today?\n- What topics are referenced but unexplored (knowledge gaps)?\n- Are my investment theses holding?\n- Which projects are stalled?\n- What are the core/bridge concepts in my knowledge graph?\n\nI need a programmatic analyst that runs on schedule, computes these insights, and writes them back as structured notes — closing the loop between accumulation and synthesis.\n\n## Tech Stack\n- Python 3.10+\n- networkx (graph analysis)\n- rapidfuzz (text similarity)\n- scikit-learn (TF-IDF/clustering optional)\n- pyyaml, markdown-it-py\n- typer, rich (CLI)\n- jinja2 (report templates)\n- sqlite-vec (semantic search if available)\n\n## Core Features\n\n### 1. Daily Knowledge Synthesis (00:30 cron)\n- New concepts, new connections formed, tag velocity, note growth stats\n- Output: `Insight: Daily Synthesis YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #daily-synthesis)\n\n### 2. Knowledge Gap Detection (weekly)\n- Broken wiki-links/aliases referencing non-existent notes\n- Inline #tags with few notes\n- Concepts mentioned in content but no dedicated note\n- Output: `Insight: Knowledge Gaps Week N` (#insight #knowledge-gap)\n\n### 3. Investment Thesis Validation (daily 20:30)\n- Reads #investment notes, extracts thesis/catalysts/targets\n- Compares with current market data (via API)\n- Logs verification\n- Output: `Insight: Thesis Validation YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #investment-validation)\n\n### 4. Project Health Dashboard (18:30)\n- Scans #project/* and #devlog #issue notes\n- Computes open/closed ratio, velocity (issues/week), stale issues (>14 days), blocker count\n- Output: `Insight: Project Health YYYY-MM-DD` (#insight #project-health)\n\n### 5. Graph Centrality Analysis (weekly)\n- networkx on wiki-link graph\n- PageRank (core concepts), betweenness (bridge notes), communities (topic clusters), orphans (isolated notes)\n- Output: `Insight: Graph Topology Week N` (#insight #graph-analysis)\n\n### 6. Tag Drift Report (monthly)\n- Tag co-occurrence matrix\n- Tags that merged/split\n- Orphan tags (≥1 note, no co-occurrence)\n- New tag emergence\n- Output: `Insight: Tag Taxonomy YYYY-MM` (#insight #tag-drift)\n\n### 7. Unified Output Format\n- All insights written as structured Markdown notes with frontmatter:\n - title, tags, created, insight_type, metrics_json\n- Auto-indexed by notebase, rendered by [[notebase-publish]]\n\n### 8. CLI Interface\n- `notebase-insight run --type daily|weekly|monthly|all`\n- `notebase-insight serve` (API for ad-hoc queries)\n- `notebase-insight config`\n\n## File Structure\n```\nnotebase-insight/\n├── pyproject.toml\n├── requirements.txt\n├── README.md\n├── notebase_insight/\n│ ├── __init__.py\n│ ├── __main__.py\n│ ├── cli.py\n│ ├── config.py\n│ ├── vault.py # Vault parsing (reuse notebase models)\n│ ├── graph.py # networkx graph construction & analysis\n│ ├── synthesis.py # Daily synthesis\n│ ├── gaps.py # Knowledge gap detection\n│ ├── validation.py # Thesis validation\n│ ├── health.py # Project health dashboard\n│ ├── tag_drift.py # Tag drift analysis\n│ └── templates/\n│ ├── daily_synthesis.md\n│ ├── knowledge_gaps.md\n│ ├── thesis_validation.md\n│ ├── project_health.md\n│ ├── graph_topology.md\n│ └── tag_drift.md\n└── tests/\n```\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `notebase-insight run --type daily` executes in < 60 seconds for 1000+ note vault\n- [ ] Daily synthesis note created with: new notes count, new tags, top connected concepts, tag velocity chart data\n- [ ] Knowledge gap note lists: broken wiki-links (top 20), orphan tags, concepts with ≥3 mentions but no note\n- [ ] Thesis validation note shows: thesis, target, current price, % to target, catalyst status (hit/miss/pending), verdict\n- [ ] Project health note shows: project name, open issues, closed this week, velocity, stale issues list, blockers\n- [ ] Graph topology note: top 10 PageRank notes, top 10 betweenness bridges, community clusters (label + size), orphan count\n- [ ] Tag drift note: co-occurrence heatmap data (JSON), merged/split tags, orphan tags, new tags this month\n- [ ] All output notes have proper frontmatter with insight_type, metrics_json (for programmatic reuse)\n- [ ] CLI passes --help, config loads from ~/.config/notebase-insight/config.yaml\n- [ ] Tests pass (pytest), ruff clean, mypy clean\n\n## Cron Integration\n| Time | Job | Insight Type | Tags |\n|------|-----|--------------|------|\n| 00:30 | Daily Synthesis | daily | #insight #daily-synthesis |\n| 18:30 | Project Health | daily | #insight #project-health |\n| 20:30 | Thesis Validation | daily | #insight #investment-validation |\n| Sat 09:30 | Knowledge Gaps + Graph Topology | weekly | #insight #knowledge-gap #graph-analysis |\n| 1st of month 09:30 | Tag Drift | monthly | #insight #tag-drift |\n\n## Related Notes\n- [[notebase-publish]] — Public cognitive exoskeleton (renders these insights)\n- [[dev_cycle_state]] — Tracks current slug='spec-notebase-semantic-search', content='\n# SPEC: notebase-semantic-search — Local-First Semantic Search for notebase\n\n## 🎯 Project Name & One-Liner\n**notebase-semantic-search** — A lightweight semantic search server that embeds all notebase vault notes using local embeddings (via sentence-transformers/ONNX) and sqlite-vec, exposing a REST API for natural-language queries. Zero external dependencies, fully local, privacy-first.\n\n## 🧠 Problem It Solves / Why Interesting\n- **Current gap**: notebase has fuzzy text search (rapidfuzz) but no *semantic* search — can\'t find "notes about AI safety" if they don\'t contain those exact words\n- **notebase-publish search** is client-side Fuse.js on pre-built JSON — same limitation, no semantic understanding\n- **sqlite-vec** provides a perfect local vector DB: single-file, SQL-native, no server, ~200KB extension\n- **Integration opportunity**: Reuse notebase\'s vault parser (`vault.py`), embed notes on write or batch, serve API for both CLI and web UI\n- **Future RAG foundation**: This becomes the retrieval layer for local LLM Q&A over the vault\n\n## 🛠 Tech Stack\n- **Python 3.10+** (matches notebase/notebase-insight)\n- **sqlite-vec** v0.1.10+ (vector search extension)\n- **sentence-transformers** or **fastembed** (ONNX embeddings, no PyTorch at runtime)\n- **FastAPI** (REST API, async, auto OpenAPI docs)\n- **pydantic** (request/response validation)\n- **Typer** (CLI for index/reindex/serve commands)\n- **rich** (CLI output)\n- **notebase.vault** (reused parser — import from sibling project)\n\n## ✨ Core Features (4-5 bullets)\n1. **Indexer**: Walks vault, chunks notes (by heading/paragraph), computes embeddings, stores in sqlite-vec virtual table with metadata (note_id, title, tags, chunk_text, chunk_idx, created)\n2. **REST API** (`/search`, `/search/hybrid`, `/health`, `/stats`): Semantic KNN search with optional metadata filters (tags, date range), returns ranked results with snippets\n3. **Hybrid Search**: Combine sqlite-vec vector similarity + rapidfuzz text score (configurable weight) for best of both worlds\n4. **Incremental Updates**: Watch vault for changes (file mtime), re-embed only modified notes; `reindex` command for full rebuild\n5. **CLI**: `notebase-semantic-search index`, `serve --port 8080`, `reindex`, `stats`, `config`\n\n## 📁 File Structure Plan\n```\nnotebase-semantic-search/\n├── pyproject.toml\n├── requirements.txt\n├── README.md\n├── notebase_semantic_search/\n│ ├── __init__.py\n│ ├── __main__.py\n│ ├── cli.py # Typer CLI: index, serve, reindex, stats, config\n│ ├── config.py # YAML config (~/.config/notebase-semantic-search/)\n│ ├── indexer.py # Vault walker, chunker, embedder, sqlite-vec writer\n│ ├── search.py # Vector search + hybrid search logic\n│ ├── api.py # FastAPI app + routes\n│ ├── models.py # Pydantic models (SearchRequest, SearchResult, Chunk, NoteMeta)\n│ ├── embeddings.py # Embedding provider abstraction (sentence-transformers/fastembed)\n│ └── vault_adapter.py # Thin wrapper importing notebase.vault for note iteration\n├── tests/\n│ ├── test_indexer.py\n│ ├── test_search.py\n│ └── test_hybrid.py\n└── scripts/\n └── install_sqlite_vec.sh # Helper for loading extension\n```\n\n## ✅ Acceptance Criteria\n1. `notebase-semantic-search index --vault ~/dev_notes` completes in < 60s for 500+ notes\n2. `notebase-semantic-search serve --port 8080` starts API with OpenAPI docs at `/docs`\n3. `GET /search?q=AI+safety+risk&limit=10` returns ranked results with note_title, chunk_snippet, score, tags\n4. `GET /search/hybrid?q=investment+thesis&tag=investment&limit=5` filters by tag + semantic search\n5. Incremental update: modify one note, run index again → only that note re-embedded (< 5s)\n6. Hybrid search: `alpha=0.7` vector + `0.3` text produces better recall than either alone (manual eval)\n6. All tests pass (`pytest -q`), ruff clean, mypy clean\n7. Works with notebase-publish: add search JS widget that calls local API (CORS enabled)\n\n## 🔗 Integration Points\n- **Reads**: notebase vault (`~/dev_notes`) — uses notebase\'s Vault/Store for parsing\n- **Writes**: sqlite-vec DB (`~/.local/share/notebase-semantic-search/vectors.db`)\n- **Serves**: REST API on `localhost:8080` (configurable)\n- **Future**: notebase-publish `search.html` can call this API for semantic results\n- **Future**: notebase-insight can use semantic search for "related concepts" detection\n\n## 📋 Implementation Notes\n- **Embedding model**: `sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2` (384 dim, fast, good quality) or `BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5` (384 dim, better). Use ONNX via `fastembed` for zero-PyTorch runtime.\n- **Chunking strategy**: Split by markdown headings (`##`, `###`), then by paragraphs. Max chunk ~500 tokens. Store `chunk_idx` for reconstruction.\n- **sqlite-vec schema**:\n ```sql\n CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE chunks USING vec0(\n embedding FLOAT[384],\n note_id TEXT,\n title TEXT,\n tags TEXT, -- JSON array\n chunk_text TEXT,\n chunk_idx INTEGER,\n created TIMESTAMP,\n +partition_key=note_id\n );\n ```\n- **Distance metric**: Cosine (default for normalized embeddings)\n- **Hybrid scoring**: `score = alpha * (1 - vec_distance) + (1 - alpha) * text_score_normalized`\n- **Config file** (`~/.config/notebase-semantic-search/config.yaml`):\n ```yaml\n vault_path: "~/dev_notes"\n db_path: "~/.local/share/notebase-semantic-search/vectors.db"\n embedding_model: "BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5"\n chunk_size: 500\n chunk_overlap: 50\n hybrid_alpha: 0.7\n api_host: "0.0.0.0"\n api_port: 8080\n ```\n\n## 🚀 Next Steps (Phase 2)\n1. Scaffold project structure + pyproject.toml\n2. Implement config.py + vault_adapter.py (import notebase.vault)\n3. Implement embeddings.py with fastembed ONNX\n4. Implement indexer.py with chunking + sqlite-vec writes\n5. Implement search.py (vector + hybrid)\n6. Implement api.py (FastAPI routes)\n7. Implement cli.py (Typer commands)\n8. Write tests, verify end-to-end\n\n---\n\n*Created during Phase 1 (12:00) of daily dev cycle — 2026-06-11*', html='<h1>SPEC: notebase-semantic-search — Local-First Semantic Search for notebase</h1>\n<h2>🎯 Project Name &amp; One-Liner</h2>\n<p><strong>notebase-semantic-search</strong> — A lightweight semantic search server that embeds all notebase vault notes using local embeddings (via sentence-transformers/ONNX) and sqlite-vec, exposing a REST API for natural-language queries. Current search is keyword-based (rapidfuzz). For deeper exploration — "find all notes about transformer architecture from the last 3 months that mention attention mechanisms" — users need:\n1. **Semantic search** (vector embeddings) for conceptual queries\n2. **SQL** for precise filtering (date ranges, tag combinations, frontmatter fields)\n3. **Interactive exploration** — refine queries iteratively, see results, drill down\n\nThis tool bridges the gap: it embeds all notes using a local embedding model (via sentence-transformers or ONNX), stores vectors in sqlite-vec, and provides a `sqlite3`-style REPL + optional TUI where you can mix natural language ("notes about attention") with SQL (`WHERE tags LIKE \'%ai%\' AND created > \'2026-01-01\'`).\n\n## Why Interesting\n\n- Extends notebase without modifying it — pure companion tool\n- Demonstrates sqlite-vec + embedding pipeline practical use\n- Useful beyond notebase: any markdown vault, Obsidian, Logseq, etc.\n- MCP server mode → LLMs can query your knowledge base semantically\n\n## Tech Stack\n\n- **Python 3.10+**: Core language\n- **sqlite-vec**: Vector extension for SQLite (loadable extension)\n- **sentence-transformers** or **fastembed**: Local embeddings (no API keys)\n- **textual**: TUI for interactive mode (same as notebase)\n- **typer/rich**: CLI interface\n- **markdown-it-py**: Parse frontmatter + content (shared with notebase)\n- **pyyaml**: Frontmatter parsing\n\n## Core Features (5)\n\n1. **Indexer**: `vec-explorer index --vault ~/dev_notes` — walks vault, extracts frontmatter + content, generates embeddings, upserts into `notes.db` (SQLite + sqlite-vec)\n2. **SQL REPL**: `vec-explorer query` — interactive SQL prompt with `.schema`, `.help`, autocomplete; results as rich tables\n3. **Semantic Search**: `vec-explorer search "transformer attention"` — natural language → embedding → ANN search → ranked results with snippets\n4. **Hybrid Queries**: Mix SQL + vector in same query: `SELECT title, vec_distance(content_vec, ?) AS dist FROM notes WHERE tags LIKE \'%ai%\' ORDER BY dist LIMIT 10`\n5. **MCP Server**: `vec-explorer serve` — exposes `search`, `query`, `get_note` tools via MCP for LLM agents\n\n## File Structure Plan\n\n```\nsqlite-vec-explorer/\n├── pyproject.toml / requirements.txt\n├── README.md\n├── vec_explorer/\n│ ├── __init__.py\n│ ├── __main__.py\n│ ├── cli.py # Typer commands: index, query, search, serve\n│ ├── db.py # SQLite + sqlite-vec setup, schema, migrations\n│ ├── embedder.py # Embedding model abstraction (sentence-transformers / fastembed)\n│ ├── indexer.py # Vault walker, chunking, embedding, upsert\n│ ├── repl.py # SQL REPL with readline/history/autocomplete\n│ ├── tui.py # Textual TUI: search pane, results, note preview\n│ ├── mcp_server.py # FastMCP server exposing tools\n│ └── models.py # Pydantic models for notes, chunks, search results\n├── tests/\n│ ├── test_indexer.py\n│ ├── test_embedder.py\n│ └── test_repl.py\n└── scripts/\n └── init_db.sql\n```\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n- [ ] `vec-explorer index --vault ~/dev_notes` completes in < 60s for 1000 notes, creates `notes.db` with `notes` and `chunks` tables + vec index\n- [ ] `vec-explorer search "concept"` returns top-10 semantic matches with snippets in < 500ms\n- [ ] `vec-explorer query` launches REPL: `.tables`, `SELECT * FROM notes LIMIT 5` works, TAB autocomplete on table/column names\n- [ ] Hybrid query works: `SELECT title, vec_distance(chunk_vec, vec_tab(?)) FROM chunks WHERE note_tags LIKE \'%ai%\' ORDER BY 2 LIMIT 5`\n- [ ] `vec-explorer serve` starts MCP server; `mcp-cli` can call `search({query: "attention"})`\n- [ ] TUI (`vec-explorer tui`) launches: search box, results list, note preview pane, `q` to quit\n- [ ] Ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest passes\n- [ ] README with install, quickstart, examples\n\n## Related\n\n- Builds on: notebase vault structure, sqlite-vec skill\n- Enables: LLM agents querying my knowledge base via MCP\n- Next: Could add re-ranking (cross-encoder), hybrid BM25+vector, graph RAG', html="<h1>Project: sqlite-vec-explorer</h1>\n<p><strong>One-liner</strong>: An interactive CLI + TUI tool that combines SQL queries with sqlite-vec semantic search over the notebase vault — query your knowledge base with natural language AND structured SQL.</p>\n<h2>Problem It Solves</h2>\n<p>The notebase vault grows daily with hundreds of notes. Current search is keyword-based (rapidfuzz). For deeper exploration — "find all notes about transformer architecture from the last 3 months that mention attention mechanisms" — users need:\n1. **Semantic search** (vector embeddings) for conceptual queries\n2. **SQL** for precise filtering (date ranges, tag combinations, frontmatter fields)\n3. **Interactive exploration** — refine queries iteratively, see results, drill down\n\nThis tool bridges the gap: it embeds all notes using a local embedding model (via sentence-transformers or ONNX), stores vectors in sqlite-vec, and provides a `sqlite3`-style REPL + optional TUI where you can mix natural language ("notes about attention") with SQL (`WHERE tags LIKE \'%ai%\' AND created > \'2026-01-01\'`).\n\n## Why Interesting\n\n- Extends notebase without modifying it — pure companion tool\n- Demonstrates sqlite-vec + embedding pipeline practical use\n- Useful beyond notebase: any markdown vault, Obsidian, Logseq, etc.\n- MCP server mode → LLMs can query your knowledge base semantically\n\n## Tech Stack\n\n- **Python 3.10+**: Core language\n- **sqlite-vec**: Vector extension for SQLite (loadable extension)\n- **sentence-transformers** or **fastembed**: Local embeddings (no API keys)\n- **textual**: TUI for interactive mode (same as notebase)\n- **typer/rich**: CLI interface\n- **markdown-it-py**: Parse frontmatter + content (shared with notebase)\n- **pyyaml**: Frontmatter parsing\n\n## Core Features (5)\n\n1. **Indexer**: `vec-explorer index --vault ~/dev_notes` — walks vault, extracts frontmatter + content, generates embeddings, upserts into `notes.db` (SQLite + sqlite-vec)\n2. **SQL REPL**: `vec-explorer query` — interactive SQL prompt with `.schema`, `.help`, autocomplete; results as rich tables\n3. **Semantic Search**: `vec-explorer search "transformer attention"` — natural language → embedding → ANN search → ranked results with snippets\n4. **Hybrid Queries**: Mix SQL + vector in same query: `SELECT title, vec_distance(content_vec, ?) AS dist FROM notes WHERE tags LIKE \'%ai%\' ORDER BY dist LIMIT 10`\n5. **MCP Server**: `vec-explorer serve` — exposes `search`, `query`, `get_note` tools via MCP for LLM agents\n\n## File Structure Plan\n\n```\nsqlite-vec-explorer/\n├── pyproject.toml / requirements.txt\n├── README.md\n├── vec_explorer/\n│ ├── __init__.py\n│ ├── __main__.py\n│ ├── cli.py # Typer commands: index, query, search, serve\n│ ├── db.py # SQLite + sqlite-vec setup, schema, migrations\n│ ├── embedder.py # Embedding model abstraction (sentence-transformers / fastembed)\n│ ├── indexer.py # Vault walker, chunking, embedding, upsert\n│ ├── repl.py # SQL REPL with readline/history/autocomplete\n│ ├── tui.py # Textual TUI: search pane, results, note preview\n│ ├── mcp_server.py # FastMCP server exposing tools\n│ └── models.py # Pydantic models for notes, chunks, search results\n├── tests/\n│ ├── test_indexer.py\n│ ├── test_embedder.py\n│ └── test_repl.py\n└── scripts/\n └── init_db.sql\n```\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n- [ ] `vec-explorer index --vault ~/dev_notes` completes in < 60s for 1000 notes, creates `notes.db` with `notes` and `chunks` tables + vec index\n- [ ] `vec-explorer search "concept"` returns top-10 semantic matches with snippets in < 500ms\n- [ ] `vec-explorer query` launches REPL: `.tables`, `SELECT * FROM notes LIMIT 5` works, TAB autocomplete on table/column names\n- [ ] Hybrid query works: `SELECT title, vec_distance(chunk_vec, vec_tab(?)) FROM chunks WHERE note_tags LIKE \'%ai%\' ORDER BY 2 LIMIT 5`\n- [ ] `vec-explorer serve` starts MCP server; `mcp-cli` can call `search({query: "attention"})`\n- [ ] TUI (`vec-explorer tui`) launches: search box, results list, note preview pane, `q` to quit\n- [ ] Ruff clean, mypy clean, pytest passes\n- [ ] README with install, quickstart, examples\n\n## Related\n\n- Builds on: notebase vault structure, sqlite-vec skill\n- Enables: LLM agents querying my knowledge base via MCP\n- Next: Could add re-ranking (cross-encoder), hybrid BM25+vector, graph RAG', html="<h1>Project: sqlite-vec-explorer</h1>\n<p><strong>One-liner</strong>: An interactive CLI + TUI tool that combines SQL queries with sqlite-vec semantic search over the notebase vault — query your knowledge base with natural language AND structured SQL.</p>\n<h2>Problem It Solves</h2>\n<p>The notebase vault grows daily with hundreds of notes. 關鍵摘要: v0.1.0 穩定版發布，支援 100萬 128維向量、二進制量化、Matryoshka Embeddings、多語言 SDK\n- [SQLite-vec 深度解析 (Generative AI)](https://generativeai.pub/sqlite-vec-a-powerful-extremely-small-fast-enough-vector-search-sqlite-extension-cd1a0747e9d3) - 關鍵摘要: 完整 Python 範例、基準測試方法、未來路線圖（元資料過濾、ANN 索引、分區儲存）\n- [GitHub Repository (asg017/sqlite-vec)](https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec) - 關鍵摘要: 7.7k stars，v0.1.10-alpha.4 最新版，支援多種索引類型、命令列、partition keys、Metadata filtering\n- [Official Python Guide](https://alexgarcia.xyz/sqlite-vec/python.html) - 關鍵摘要: macOS 關鍵問題、序列化方法、SQLite 版本需求、pysqlite3 替代方案\n- [Official Installation Guide](https://alexgarcia.xyz/sqlite-vec/installation.html) - 關鍵摘要: 所有語言安裝指令、預編譯擴展下載、編譯選項\n\n## 核心概念\nsqlite-vec 是一個輕量級、可移植的 SQLite 擴展，通過虛擬表 實現向量搜索。關鍵特點：\n- **零依賴**：單一 C 擴展檔，~200KB 編譯後\n- **跨平台**：Linux/macOS/Windows/WASM/行動裝置/嵌入式\n- **SQL 原生**：使用標準 SQL INSERT/SELECT/MATCH 進行向量操作\n- **多向量類型**：float[D]、int8[D]、bit[D] (二進制量化)\n- **多索引類型**：Flat (暴力搜索)、IVF (倒排檔案)、DiskANN (圖基於磁盤)、Rescore (兩階段)\n\n核心架構：\n1. vec0 虛擬表：主要介面，支援向量欄位 + 輔助欄位 + partition keys\n2. 影子表：自動管理的 _vec_chunks、_metadata_chunks、_ivf_*、_diskann_*\n3. 隱藏欄位：distance、k、command (v0.1.10+ FTS5 風格運行時命令)\n\n## 適用場景\n- **本地優先 RAG**：文檔塊 + embedding 存儲，零資料外洩\n- **語義搜索**：產品/代碼/內容搜索，使用 embeddings\n- **推薦系統**：item/user embedding 相似度\n- **邊緣/移動 AI**：瀏覽器、樹莓派、行動裝置完全本地運行\n- **隱私優先應用**：所有資料永不離開設備\n- **原型開發**：熟悉的 SQL，零基礎設施\n\n## 關鍵 API / 指令\n```bash\n# 安裝\npip install sqlite-vec # Python\nnpm install sqlite-vec # Node.js\ncargo add sqlite-vec # Rust\ngo get -u github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec-go-bindings/cgo # Go\n\n# 預編譯擴展通用安裝\ncurl -o install.sh -L https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec/releases/latest/download/install.sh\n./install.sh\n\n# Python 基礎用法\npython -c "\nimport sqlite3, sqlite_vec\ndb = sqlite3.connect(\':memory:\')\ndb.enable_load_extension(True)\nsqlite_vec.load(db)\ndb.enable_load_extension(False)\nprint(db.execute(\'select vec_version()\').fetchone())\n"\n\n# 創建向量表\nCREATE VIRTUAL TABLE docs USING vec0(\n embedding FLOAT[384],\n category TEXT,\n +partition_key=category\n);\n\n# 插入向量 (Python 列表需序列化)\nfrom sqlite_vec import serialize_float32\nembedding = [0.1] * 384\nINSERT INTO docs(embedding, category) VALUES (serialize_float32(embedding), \'tech\');\n\n# KNN 相似度搜索\nSELECT category, distance FROM docs\nWHERE embedding MATCH ? ORDER BY distance LIMIT 5;\n\n# 二進制量化 (32x 空間減少)\nSELECT vec_quantize_binary(embedding) FROM docs;\n\n# 運行時命令\nINSERT INTO docs(docs) VALUES (\'oversample=32\');\n```\n\n## 常見坑點\n1. **macOS 系統 Python 擴展被阻擋** - AttributeError: \'sqlite3.Connection\' object has no attribute \'enable_load_extension\' - 解決: 使用 Homebrew Python (brew install python) 或 pysqlite3\n2. **NumPy 必須用 float32** - embeddings 預設 float64 會導致錯誤 - 解決: embedding.astype(np.float32)\n3. **Python 列表必須序列化** - 直接傳遞列表會報錯 - 解決: serialize_float32([0.1, 0.2, ...])\n4. **SQLite 版本 < 3.41** - 某些功能失效 - 解決: pip install pysqlite3 或升級 Python\n5. **Windows 預編譯二進制選錯** - 下載 tar.gz 而非 cvec0.all debug 檔案\n6. **DiskANN/IVF 預設未啟用** - 需編譯時旗標 - 解決: Docker 或從源碼編譯\n7. **Pre-v1 破壞性變更** - 版本間可能有 breaking changes - 解決: 鎖定版本 sqlite-vec==0.1.10-alpha.4\n8. **向量維度限制 8192** - 硬編碼限制 - 解決: 從源碼編譯修改限制\n\n## 關聯技能\n- llama-cpp: 本地 GGUF 推理，配合 sqlite-vec 實現全本地 RAG\n- serving-llms-vllm: 高吞吐 LLM 服務，可作為 embedding 生成後端\n- evaluating-llms-harness: 基準測試工具，可評估 RAG 檢索質量\n- sqlite-vec: (本技能) 向量搜索核心能力\n', html='<h2>學習來源</h2>\n<ul>\n<li><a 進行向量操作</li>\n<li><strong>多向量類型</strong>：float[D]、int8[D]、bit[D] (二進制量化)</li>\n<li><strong>多索引類型</strong>：Flat (暴力搜索)、IVF (倒排檔案)、DiskANN (圖基於磁盤)、Rescore (兩階段)</li>\n</ul>\n<p>核心架構：</p>\n<ol>\n<li>vec0 風格運行時命令)</li>\n</ol>\n<h2>適用場景</h2>\n<ul>\n<li><strong>本地優先 RAG</strong>：文檔塊 + embedding 存儲，零資料外洩</li>\n<li><strong>語義搜索</strong>：產品/代碼/內容搜索，使用 embeddings</li>\n<li><strong>推薦系統</strong>：item/user embedding 相似度</li>\n<li><strong>邊緣/移動 AI</strong>：瀏覽器、樹莓派、行動裝置完全本地運行</li>\n<li><strong>隱私優先應用</strong>：所有資料永不離開設備</li>\n<li><strong>原型開發</strong>：熟悉的 SQL，零基礎設施</li>\n</ul>\n<h2>關鍵 API / 指令</h2>\n<pre><code class="language-bash"># 安裝\npip install sqlite-vec # Python\nnpm install sqlite-vec # Node.js\ncargo add sqlite-vec # Rust\ngo get -u github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec-go-bindings/cgo # Go\n\n# 預編譯擴展通用安裝\ncurl -o install.sh -L https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec/releases/latest/download/install.sh\n./install.sh\n\n# Python 基礎用法\npython -c &quot;\nimport sqlite3, sqlite_vec\ndb = sqlite3.connect(\':memory:\')\ndb.enable_load_extension(True)\nsqlite_vec.load(db)\ndb.enable_load_extension(False)\nprint(db.execute(\'select vec_version()\').fetchone())\n&quot;\n\n# 創建向量表\nCREATE VIRTUAL TABLE docs USING vec0(\n embedding FLOAT[384],\n category TEXT,\n +partition_key=category\n);\n\n# 插入向量 (Python 列表需序列化)\nfrom sqlite_vec import serialize_float32\nembedding = [0.1] * 384\nINSERT INTO docs(embedding, category) VALUES (serialize_float32(embedding), \'tech\');\n\n# KNN 相似度搜索\nSELECT category, distance FROM docs\nWHERE embedding MATCH ? ORDER BY distance LIMIT 5;\n\n# 二進制量化 (32x 空間減少)\nSELECT vec_quantize_binary(embedding) FROM docs;\n\n# 運行時命令\nINSERT INTO docs(docs) VALUES (\'oversample=32\');\n</code></pre>\n<h2>常見坑點</h2>\n<ol>\n<li><strong>macOS 系統 Python 擴展被阻擋</strong> - AttributeError: \'sqlite3.Connection\' object has no attribute \'enable_load_extension\' - 解決: 使用 Homebrew Python (brew install python) 或 pysqlite3</li>\n<li><strong>NumPy 必須用 float32</strong> - embeddings 預設 float64 會導致錯誤 - 解決: embedding.astype(np.float32)</li>\n<li><strong>Python 列表必須序列化</strong> - 直接傳遞列表會報錯 - 解決: serialize_float32([0.1, 0.2, ...])</li>\n<li><strong>SQLite 版本 &lt; 3.41</strong> - 某些功能失效 - 解決: pip install pysqlite3 或升級 Python</li>\n<li><strong>Windows 預編譯二進制選錯</strong> - 下載 tar.gz 而非 cvec0.all debug 檔案</li>\n<li><strong>DiskANN/IVF 預設未啟用</strong> - 需編譯時旗標 - 解決: Docker 或從源碼編譯</li>\n<li><strong>Pre-v1 破壞性變更</strong> - 版本間可能有 breaking changes - 解決: 鎖定版本 sqlite-vec==0.1.10-alpha.4</li>\n<li><strong>向量維度限制 8192</strong> - 硬編碼限制 - 解決: 從源碼編譯修改限制</li>\n</ol>\n<h2>關聯技能</h2>\n<ul>\n<li>llama-cpp: 本地 GGUF 推理，配合 sqlite-vec 實現全本地 RAG</li>\n<li>serving-llms-vllm: 高吞吐 LLM 服務，可作為 embedding 生成後端</li>\n<li>evaluating-llms-harness: 基準測試工具，可評估 RAG 檢索質量</li>\n<li>sqlite-vec: (本技能) 向量搜索核心能力</li>\n</ul>\n', tags=['mlops', 'local-ai', 'sqlite-vec', 'rag', 'skill-learning', 'vector-search'], aliases=[], created=datetime.datetime(2026, 6, 11, 0, 20, 39, 823072), modified=datetime.datetime(2026, 6, 11, 0, 20, 39, 823088), frontmatter={'title': 'Skill Learning: sqlite-vec', 'tags': ['skill-learning', 'mlops', 'sqlite-vec', 'vector-search', 'rag', 'local-ai'], 'aliases': [], 'created': '2026-06-11T00:20:39.823072', 'modified': '2026-06-11T00:20:39.823088'}, wiki_links=[])] #skill-learning [Note(path=PosixPath('/home/11405611/dev_notes/Skill-Learning-sqlite-vec.md'), title='Skill Learning: sqlite-vec', slug='skill-learning-sqlite-vec', content='\n## 學習來源\n- [sqlite-vec v0.1.0 Released (MarkTechPost)](https://www.marktechpost.com/2024/08/04/sqlite-vec-v0-1-0-released-portable-vector-database-extension-for-sqlite-with-support-for-1-million-128-dimensional-vectors-binary-quantization-and-extensive-sdks/) - 關鍵摘要: v0.1.0 穩定版發布，支援 100萬 128維向量、二進制量化、Matryoshka Embeddings、多語言 SDK\n- [SQLite-vec 深度解析 (Generative AI)](https://generativeai.pub/sqlite-vec-a-powerful-extremely-small-fast-enough-vector-search-sqlite-extension-cd1a0747e9d3) - 關鍵摘要: 完整 Python 範例、基準測試方法、未來路線圖（元資料過濾、ANN 索引、分區儲存）\n- [GitHub Repository (asg017/sqlite-vec)](https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec) - 關鍵摘要: 7.7k stars，v0.1.10-alpha.4 最新版，支援多種索引類型、命令列、partition keys、Metadata filtering\n- [Official Python Guide](https://alexgarcia.xyz/sqlite-vec/python.html) - 關鍵摘要: macOS 關鍵問題、序列化方法、SQLite 版本需求、pysqlite3 替代方案\n- [Official Installation Guide](https://alexgarcia.xyz/sqlite-vec/installation.html) - 關鍵摘要: 所有語言安裝指令、預編譯擴展下載、編譯選項\n\n## 核心概念\nsqlite-vec 是一個輕量級、可移植的 SQLite 擴展，通過虛擬表 實現向量搜索。關鍵特點：\n- **零依賴**：單一 C 擴展檔，~200KB 編譯後\n- **跨平台**：Linux/macOS/Windows/WASM/行動裝置/嵌入式\n- **SQL 原生**：使用標準 SQL INSERT/SELECT/MATCH 進行向量操作\n- **多向量類型**：float[D]、int8[D]、bit[D] (二進制量化)\n- **多索引類型**：Flat (暴力搜索)、IVF (倒排檔案)、DiskANN (圖基於磁盤)、Rescore (兩階段)\n\n核心架構：\n1. vec0 虛擬表：主要介面，支援向量欄位 + 輔助欄位 + partition keys\n2. 影子表：自動管理的 _vec_chunks、_metadata_chunks、_ivf_*、_diskann_*\n3. 隱藏欄位：distance、k、command (v0.1.10+ FTS5 風格運行時命令)\n\n## 適用場景\n- **本地優先 RAG**：文檔塊 + embedding 存儲，零資料外洩\n- **語義搜索**：產品/代碼/內容搜索，使用 embeddings\n- **推薦系統**：item/user embedding 相似度\n- **邊緣/移動 AI**：瀏覽器、樹莓派、行動裝置完全本地運行\n- **隱私優先應用**：所有資料永不離開設備\n- **原型開發**：熟悉的 SQL，零基礎設施\n\n## 關鍵 API / 指令\n```bash\n# 安裝\npip install sqlite-vec # Python\nnpm install sqlite-vec # Node.js\ncargo add sqlite-vec # Rust\ngo get -u github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec-go-bindings/cgo # Go\n\n# 預編譯擴展通用安裝\ncurl -o install.sh -L https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec/releases/latest/download/install.sh\n./install.sh\n\n# Python 基礎用法\npython -c "\nimport sqlite3, sqlite_vec\ndb = sqlite3.connect(\':memory:\')\ndb.enable_load_extension(True)\nsqlite_vec.load(db)\ndb.enable_load_extension(False)\nprint(db.execute(\'select vec_version()\').fetchone())\n"\n\n# 創建向量表\nCREATE VIRTUAL TABLE docs USING vec0(\n embedding FLOAT[384],\n category TEXT,\n +partition_key=category\n);\n\n# 插入向量 (Python 列表需序列化)\nfrom sqlite_vec import serialize_float32\nembedding = [0.1] * 384\nINSERT INTO docs(embedding, category) VALUES (serialize_float32(embedding), \'tech\');\n\n# KNN 相似度搜索\nSELECT category, distance FROM docs\nWHERE embedding MATCH ? ORDER BY distance LIMIT 5;\n\n# 二進制量化 (32x 空間減少)\nSELECT vec_quantize_binary(embedding) FROM docs;\n\n# 運行時命令\nINSERT INTO docs(docs) VALUES (\'oversample=32\');\n```\n\n## 常見坑點\n1. **macOS 系統 Python 擴展被阻擋** - AttributeError: \'sqlite3.Connection\' object has no attribute \'enable_load_extension\' - 解決: 使用 Homebrew Python (brew install python) 或 pysqlite3\n2. **NumPy 必須用 float32** - embeddings 預設 float64 會導致錯誤 - 解決: embedding.astype(np.float32)\n3. **Python 列表必須序列化** - 直接傳遞列表會報錯 - 解決: serialize_float32([0.1, 0.2, ...])\n4. **SQLite 版本 < 3.41** - 某些功能失效 - 解決: pip install pysqlite3 或升級 Python\n5. **Windows 預編譯二進制選錯** - 下載 tar.gz 而非 cvec0.all debug 檔案\n6. **DiskANN/IVF 預設未啟用** - 需編譯時旗標 - 解決: Docker 或從源碼編譯\n7. **Pre-v1 破壞性變更** - 版本間可能有 breaking changes - 解決: 鎖定版本 sqlite-vec==0.1.10-alpha.4\n8. **向量維度限制 8192** - 硬編碼限制 - 解決: 從源碼編譯修改限制\n\n## 關聯技能\n- llama-cpp: 本地 GGUF 推理，配合 sqlite-vec 實現全本地 RAG\n- serving-llms-vllm: 高吞吐 LLM 服務，可作為 embedding 生成後端\n- evaluating-llms-harness: 基準測試工具，可評估 RAG 檢索質量\n- sqlite-vec: (本技能) 向量搜索核心能力\n', html='<h2>學習來源</h2>\n<ul>\n<li><a 關鍵摘要: v0.1.0 穩定版發布，支援 100萬 128維向量、二進制量化、Matryoshka Embeddings、多語言 SDK\n- [SQLite-vec 深度解析 (Generative AI)](https://generativeai.pub/sqlite-vec-a-powerful-extremely-small-fast-enough-vector-search-sqlite-extension-cd1a0747e9d3) - 關鍵摘要: 完整 Python 範例、基準測試方法、未來路線圖（元資料過濾、ANN 索引、分區儲存）\n- [GitHub Repository (asg017/sqlite-vec)](https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec) - 關鍵摘要: 7.7k stars，v0.1.10-alpha.4 最新版，支援多種索引類型、命令列、partition keys、Metadata filtering\n- [Official Python Guide](https://alexgarcia.xyz/sqlite-vec/python.html) - 關鍵摘要: macOS 關鍵問題、序列化方法、SQLite 版本需求、pysqlite3 替代方案\n- [Official Installation Guide](https://alexgarcia.xyz/sqlite-vec/installation.html) - 關鍵摘要: 所有語言安裝指令、預編譯擴展下載、編譯選項\n\n## 核心概念\nsqlite-vec 是一個輕量級、可移植的 SQLite 擴展，通過虛擬表 實現向量搜索。關鍵特點：\n- **零依賴**：單一 C 擴展檔，~200KB 編譯後\n- **跨平台**：Linux/macOS/Windows/WASM/行動裝置/嵌入式\n- **SQL 原生**：使用標準 SQL INSERT/SELECT/MATCH 進行向量操作\n- **多向量類型**：float[D]、int8[D]、bit[D] (二進制量化)\n- **多索引類型**：Flat (暴力搜索)、IVF (倒排檔案)、DiskANN (圖基於磁盤)、Rescore (兩階段)\n\n核心架構：\n1. vec0 虛擬表：主要介面，支援向量欄位 + 輔助欄位 + partition keys\n2. 影子表：自動管理的 _vec_chunks、_metadata_chunks、_ivf_*、_diskann_*\n3. 隱藏欄位：distance、k、command (v0.1.10+ FTS5 風格運行時命令)\n\n## 適用場景\n- **本地優先 RAG**：文檔塊 + embedding 存儲，零資料外洩\n- **語義搜索**：產品/代碼/內容搜索，使用 embeddings\n- **推薦系統**：item/user embedding 相似度\n- **邊緣/移動 AI**：瀏覽器、樹莓派、行動裝置完全本地運行\n- **隱私優先應用**：所有資料永不離開設備\n- **原型開發**：熟悉的 SQL，零基礎設施\n\n## 關鍵 API / 指令\n```bash\n# 安裝\npip install sqlite-vec # Python\nnpm install sqlite-vec # Node.js\ncargo add sqlite-vec # Rust\ngo get -u github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec-go-bindings/cgo # Go\n\n# 預編譯擴展通用安裝\ncurl -o install.sh -L https://github.com/asg017/sqlite-vec/releases/latest/download/install.sh\n./install.sh\n\n# Python 基礎用法\npython -c "\nimport sqlite3, sqlite_vec\ndb = sqlite3.connect(\':memory:\')\ndb.enable_load_extension(True)\nsqlite_vec.load(db)\ndb.enable_load_extension(False)\nprint(db.execute(\'select vec_version()\').fetchone())\n"\n\n# 創建向量表\nCREATE VIRTUAL TABLE docs USING vec0(\n embedding FLOAT[384],\n category TEXT,\n +partition_key=category\n);\n\n# 插入向量 (Python 列表需序列化)\nfrom sqlite_vec import serialize_float32\nembedding = [0.1] * 384\nINSERT INTO docs(embedding, category) VALUES (serialize_float32(embedding), \'tech\');\n\n# KNN 相似度搜索\nSELECT category, distance FROM docs\nWHERE embedding MATCH ? ORDER BY distance LIMIT 5;\n\n# 二進制量化 (32x 空間減少)\nSELECT vec_quantize_binary(embedding) FROM docs;\n\n# 運行時命令\nINSERT INTO docs(docs) VALUES (\'oversample=32\');\n```\n\n## 常見坑點\n1. **macOS 系統 Python 擴展被阻擋** - AttributeError: \'sqlite3.Connection\' object has no attribute \'enable_load_extension\' - 解決: 使用 Homebrew Python (brew install python) 或 pysqlite3\n2. **NumPy 必須用 float32** - embeddings 預設 float64 會導致錯誤 - 解決: embedding.astype(np.float32)\n3. **Python 列表必須序列化** - 直接傳遞列表會報錯 - 解決: serialize_float32([0.1, 0.2, ...])\n4. **SQLite 版本 < 3.41** - 某些功能失效 - 解決: pip install pysqlite3 或升級 Python\n5. **Windows 預編譯二進制選錯** - 下載 tar.gz 而非 cvec0.all debug 檔案\n6. **DiskANN/IVF 預設未啟用** - 需編譯時旗標 - 解決: Docker 或從源碼編譯\n7. **Pre-v1 破壞性變更** - 版本間可能有 breaking changes - 解決: 鎖定版本 sqlite-vec==0.1.10-alpha.4\n8. **向量維度限制 8192** - 硬編碼限制 - 解決: 從源碼編譯修改限制\n\n## 關聯技能\n- llama-cpp: 本地 GGUF 推理，配合 sqlite-vec 實現全本地 RAG\n- serving-llms-vllm: 高吞吐 LLM 服務，可作為 embedding 生成後端\n- evaluating-llms-harness: 基準測試工具，可評估 RAG 檢索質量\n- sqlite-vec: (本技能) 向量搜索核心能力\n', html='<h2>學習來源</h2>\n<ul>\n<li><a v0.1.0 Released (MarkTechPost)</a> - 關鍵摘要: v0.1.0 穩定版發布，支援 100萬 128維向量、二進制量化、Matryoshka Embeddings、多語言 SDK</li>\n<li><a
